                    "speech_quirks" to "특별한 말투의 버릇은 없으며, 대화체적인 표현이 자연스럽다.",
                    "tone" to "담담하고 차분한 어조로 일상의 소소한 사건들을 서술."
                ),
                styleExample = listOf(
                    "일어나서 물을 한 잔 마셨다",
                    "조용히 하루가 지나갔다",
                    "일기를 쓰고 자야겠다고 생각했다",
                    "창문을 여니 바깥 공기가 들어왔다"
                ),
                styleVector = emptyList()
            )
        }
    }

package com.example.sumdays.daily.memo

import android.util.Log
import com.example.sumdays.daily.memo.MemoMergeUtils.convertStylePromptToMap
import com.example.sumdays.data.dao.UserStyleDao
import com.example.sumdays.network.ApiClient
import com.example.sumdays.settings.prefs.UserStatsPrefs

object MoodRepository {

    private val defaultStylePrompt: Map<String, Any> = mapOf(
        "character_concept" to "일상적인 삶을 살아가는 평범한 사람. 소소한 일상을 관찰하고 기록하는 성향을 가진 인물.",
        "emotional_tone" to "감정이 드러나지 않고 중립적인 톤으로, 일상적인 사건을 기록하는 데 집중한다.",
        "formality" to "비격식적인 대화체로, 자연스러운 흐름을 유지하며 친근한 느낌을 준다.",
        "lexical_choice" to "일상적인 단어와 표현을 사용하여 친근함을 느끼게 한다.",
        "pacing" to "느긋하고 여유로운 흐름, 빠르지 않게 사건을 나열.",
        "punctuation_style" to "기본적인 문장 부호 사용, 복잡한 구두점은 없다.",
        "sentence_endings" to listOf("~었다.", "~했다.", "~었다고 생각했다."),
        "sentence_length" to "중간 길이의 문장들이 많으며, 간결하게 표현되어 있다.",
        "sentence_structure" to "주어-서술어 구조가 명확하며, 문장이 단순하고 직관적이다.",
        "special_syntax" to "일상적인 표현을 그대로 사용하며, 특별한 구문은 없음.",
        "speech_quirks" to "특별한 말투의 버릇은 없으며, 대화체적인 표현이 자연스럽다.",
        "tone" to "담담하고 차분한 어조로 일상의 소소한 사건들을 서술."
    )

    private val defaultStyleExamples: List<String> = listOf(
        "일어나서 물을 한 잔 마셨다",
        "조용히 하루가 지나갔다",
        "일기를 쓰고 자야겠다고 생각했다",
        "창문을 여니 바깥 공기가 들어왔다"
    )

    suspend fun generateMood(
        diary: String,
        userStatsPrefs: UserStatsPrefs,
        userStyleDao: UserStyleDao
    ): String? {
        return try {
            val activeStyleId = userStatsPrefs.getActiveStyleId()
            val stored = userStyleDao.getStyleById(activeStyleId)
            val stylePrompt: Map<String, Any>
            val styleExamples: List<String>
            if (stored != null) {
                stylePrompt = convertStylePromptToMap(stored.stylePrompt)
                styleExamples = stored.styleExamples
            } else {
                stylePrompt = defaultStylePrompt
                styleExamples = defaultStyleExamples
            }
            val request = MoodRequest(
                diary = diary,
                stylePrompt = stylePrompt,
                styleExamples = styleExamples
            )
            val response = ApiClient.api.generateMood(request)
            if (!response.isSuccessful) return null
            val json = response.body() ?: return null
            json.getAsJsonObject("result")
                ?.get("mood")?.takeIf { !it.isJsonNull }?.asString
        } catch (e: kotlinx.coroutines.CancellationException) {
            throw e
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Log.e("MoodRepository", "generateMood failed", e)
            null
        }
    }
}


========== FILE: C:\Users\SAMSUNG\mingu_github\sumdays\Sumdays\app\src\main\java\com\example\sumdays\daily\memo\PendingMergeStore.kt ==========
package com.example.sumdays.daily.memo

import android.content.Context
import com.google.gson.Gson
import java.security.MessageDigest
import java.util.UUID

/** 메모 본문은 저장하지 않고 요청 해시와 ID만 보관한다. 응답 유실 시 같은 입력을 재시도한다. */
object PendingMergeStore {
    private fun prefs(context: Context) = context.getSharedPreferences("pending_merge", Context.MODE_PRIVATE)
    private fun key(userId: Int, request: MergeRequest): String {
        val digest = MessageDigest.getInstance("SHA-256")
            .digest(Gson().toJson(request.copy(requestId = "")).toByteArray(Charsets.UTF_8))
            .joinToString("") { "%02x".format(it) }
        return "user_${userId}_$digest"
    }

    @Synchronized
    fun getOrCreate(context: Context, userId: Int, request: MergeRequest): String {
        val prefs = prefs(context)
        val key = key(userId, request)
        return prefs.getString(key, null) ?: UUID.randomUUID().toString().also {
            check(prefs.edit().putString(key, it).commit()) { "합치기 요청을 저장하지 못했습니다." }
        }
    }

    fun clear(context: Context, userId: Int, request: MergeRequest) {
        prefs(context).edit().remove(key(userId, request)).apply()
    }
}


========== FILE: C:\Users\SAMSUNG\mingu_github\sumdays\Sumdays\app\src\main\java\com\example\sumdays\data\sync\BackupSyncRunner.kt ==========
    package com.example.sumdays.data.sync

    import android.content.Context
    import androidx.room.withTransaction
    import androidx.work.WorkManager
    import com.example.sumdays.auth.SessionManager
    import com.example.sumdays.data.AppDatabase
    import com.example.sumdays.network.ApiClient
    import com.example.sumdays.network.apiService.ApiService
    import kotlinx.coroutines.CancellationException
    import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
    import kotlinx.coroutines.NonCancellable
    import kotlinx.coroutines.sync.Mutex
    import kotlinx.coroutines.sync.withLock
    import kotlinx.coroutines.withContext
    import java.io.IOException
    import android.net.Uri
    import com.example.sumdays.settings.prefs.ProfileImagePrefs
    import com.google.gson.Gson
    import okhttp3.MultipartBody
    import okhttp3.MediaType.Companion.toMediaTypeOrNull
    import okhttp3.RequestBody.Companion.toRequestBody

    data class BackupSyncResult(
        val success: Boolean,
        val type: String? = null,
        val message: String? = null,
    )

    object BackupSyncRunner {
        private val syncMutex = Mutex()

        suspend fun syncNow(
            context: Context,
            api: ApiService = ApiClient.api,
            db: AppDatabase = AppDatabase.getDatabase(context.applicationContext),
            logoutAfterSync: Boolean = false,
        ): BackupSyncResult = syncMutex.withLock {
            withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
                val userId = SessionManager.getUserId()
                val token = SessionManager.getToken()
                    ?: return@withContext BackupSyncResult(false, "token_error", "token이 유효하지 않음")
                if (!LocalDataOwnerPrefs.isReadyFor(context.applicationContext, userId)) {
                    InitialSyncScheduler.ensureScheduled(context)
                    return@withContext BackupSyncResult(
                        false,
                        "account_sync_pending",
                        "계정 데이터를 준비하고 있어요. 연결되면 자동으로 재시도합니다. 잠시 후 다시 확인해 주세요.",
                    )
                }

                try {
                    val memoDao = db.memoDao()
                    val userStyleDao = db.userStyleDao()
                    val dailyEntryDao = db.dailyEntryDao()
                    val weekSummaryDao = db.weekSummaryDao()

                    val deletedMemos = memoDao.getDeletedMemos()
                    val deletedMemoIds = deletedMemos.map { it.id }
                    val editedMemos = memoDao.getEditedMemos()
                    val deletedStyles = userStyleDao.getDeletedStyles()
                    val deletedStyleIds = deletedStyles.map { it.styleId }
                    val editedStyles = userStyleDao.getEditedStyles()
                    val deletedEntries = dailyEntryDao.getDeletedEntries()
                    val deletedEntryDates = deletedEntries.map { it.date }
                    val editedEntries = dailyEntryDao.getEditedEntries()



                    // 사진
                    val dailyPhotosPayloads = mutableListOf<DailyPhotosPayload>()
                    val photoParts = mutableListOf<MultipartBody.Part>()
                    editedEntries.forEach { entry ->
                        val photos = entry.photoUrls
                            ?.split(",")
                            ?.filter { it.isNotBlank() }
                            ?: emptyList()

                        val serverPhotos = photos.filter {
                            it.startsWith("http://") || it.startsWith("https://")
                        }

                        val localPhotos = photos.filter {
                            it.startsWith("content://") || it.startsWith("file://")
                        }

                        val uploadKeys = mutableListOf<String>()

                        localPhotos.forEachIndexed { index, uriString ->
                            val uploadKey = "${entry.date}_${index}.jpg"
                            uploadKeys.add(uploadKey)

                            photoParts.add(
                                uriToMultipart(context.applicationContext, uriString, uploadKey)
                            )
                        }

                        dailyPhotosPayloads.add(
                            DailyPhotosPayload(
                                date = entry.date,
                                serverPhotoUrls = serverPhotos
                                    .takeIf { it.isNotEmpty() }
                                    ?.joinToString(","),
                                uploadKeys = uploadKeys
                                    .takeIf { it.isNotEmpty() }
                            )
                        )
                    }

                    val deletedSummaries = weekSummaryDao.getDeletedSummaries()
                    val deletedSummaryStartDates = deletedSummaries.map { it.startDate }
                    val editedSummaryEntities = weekSummaryDao.getEditedSummaries()
                    val editedSummaries = editedSummaryEntities.map { it.weekSummary }

                    var syncedPhotos: List<SyncedDailyPhotoUrlsPayload>? = null
                    val request = buildSyncRequest(
                        deletedMemoIds,
                        deletedStyleIds,
                        deletedEntryDates,
                        deletedSummaryStartDates,
                        editedMemos,
                        editedStyles,
                        editedEntries,
                        editedSummaries,
                        dailyPhotosPayloads
                    )
                    if (
                        SessionManager.getUserId() != userId ||
                        !LocalDataOwnerPrefs.isReadyFor(context.applicationContext, userId)
                    ) {
                        return@withContext BackupSyncResult(
                            false,
                            "account_changed",
                            "동기화 중 로그인 계정이 변경됨",
                        )
                    }
                    if (request.edited != null || request.deleted != null) {
                        val syncJson = Gson().toJson(request)

                        val syncRequestBody = syncJson.toRequestBody(
                            "application/json".toMediaTypeOrNull()
                        )

                        val response = api.syncData(
                            "Bearer $token",
                            syncRequestBody,
                            photoParts
                        )
                        val body = response.body()
                        if (!response.isSuccessful || body?.status != "success") {
                            return@withContext BackupSyncResult(
                                false,
                                if (response.code() in listOf(408, 429) || response.code() >= 500) "server_retry" else "server_error",
                                body?.message ?: "서버 응답 없음 (${response.code()})",
                            )
                        }
                        syncedPhotos = body.dailyPhotos
                    }

                    if (
                        SessionManager.getUserId() != userId ||
                        !LocalDataOwnerPrefs.isReadyFor(context.applicationContext, userId)
                    ) {
                        return@withContext BackupSyncResult(
                            false,
                            "account_changed",
                            "동기화 중 로그인 계정이 변경됨",
                        )
                    }

                    db.withTransaction {
                        if (
                            SessionManager.getUserId() != userId ||
                            !LocalDataOwnerPrefs.isReadyFor(context.applicationContext, userId)
                        ) {
                            throw AccountChangedException()
                        }

                        val unchangedEntryDates = dailyEntryDao.getEditedEntries()
                            .filter { it in editedEntries }
                            .map { it.date }
                            .toSet()

                        // Only acknowledge the exact snapshots uploaded. A newer
                        // local edit/deletion must remain pending for the next run.
                        memoDao.resetDeletedFlags(memoDao.getDeletedMemos().filter { it in deletedMemos }.map { it.id })
                        memoDao.resetEditedFlags(memoDao.getEditedMemos().filter { it in editedMemos }.map { it.id })
                        userStyleDao.resetDeletedFlags(userStyleDao.getDeletedStyles().filter { it in deletedStyles }.map { it.styleId })
                        userStyleDao.resetEditedFlags(userStyleDao.getEditedStyles().filter { it in editedStyles }.map { it.styleId })
                        dailyEntryDao.resetDeletedFlags(dailyEntryDao.getDeletedEntries().filter { it in deletedEntries }.map { it.date })
                        dailyEntryDao.resetEditedFlags(unchangedEntryDates.toList())
                        weekSummaryDao.resetDeletedFlags(weekSummaryDao.getDeletedSummaries().filter { it in deletedSummaries }.map { it.startDate })
                        weekSummaryDao.resetEditedFlags(weekSummaryDao.getEditedSummaries().filter { it in editedSummaryEntities }.map { it.startDate })

                        syncedPhotos?.forEach { photo ->
                            if (photo.date in unchangedEntryDates) {
                                dailyEntryDao.updatePhotoUrls(
                                    photo.date,
                                    photo.photoUrls
                                )
                            }
                        }

                        if (
                            SessionManager.getUserId() != userId ||
                            !LocalDataOwnerPrefs.isReadyFor(context.applicationContext, userId)
                        ) {
                            throw AccountChangedException()
                        }
                    }

                    if (logoutAfterSync) {
                        // Do not erase anything until every pending change is on
                        // the server. Cancellation cannot split DB/session cleanup.
                        // 기존 데이터 초기화 부분 (Room, Pref)
                        withContext(NonCancellable) {
                            // 1. Room 초기화
                            db.withTransaction {
                                if (SessionManager.getUserId() != userId || !LocalDataOwnerPrefs.isReadyFor(context, userId)) {
                                    throw AccountChangedException()
                                }
                                if (hasPendingChanges(db)) throw PendingChangesException()
                                memoDao.clearAll()
                                dailyEntryDao.clearAll()
                                userStyleDao.clearAll()
                                weekSummaryDao.clearAll()
                            }

                            // 2. Pref 초기화
                            // TODO : 다른 Pref 초기화해야함
                            LocalDataOwnerPrefs.clear(context)
                            ProfileImagePrefs.clear(context)
                            SessionManager.clearSession()


                            // 3. 미완료 작업 종료
                            WorkManager.getInstance(context).cancelAllWorkByTag(InitialSyncWorker.accountWorkTag(userId))
                        }
                    }

                    BackupSyncResult(true)
                } catch (error: CancellationException) {
                    throw error
                } catch (error: IOException) {
                    BackupSyncResult(false, "connection_error", "서버에 연결하지 못했어요. 기기의 일기는 보관되어 있습니다.")
                } catch (error: PendingChangesException) {
                    BackupSyncResult(false, "changes_pending", "새 변경사항을 저장하고 있어요. 잠시 후 다시 시도해 주세요.")
                } catch (error: Exception) {
                    BackupSyncResult(false, "exception", error.message ?: "알 수 없는 오류")
                }
            }
        }

        private suspend fun hasPendingChanges(db: AppDatabase): Boolean =
            db.dailyEntryDao().getEditedEntries().isNotEmpty() || db.dailyEntryDao().getDeletedEntries().isNotEmpty() ||
                    db.memoDao().getEditedMemos().isNotEmpty() || db.memoDao().getDeletedMemos().isNotEmpty() ||
                    db.userStyleDao().getEditedStyles().isNotEmpty() || db.userStyleDao().getDeletedStyles().isNotEmpty() ||
                    db.weekSummaryDao().getEditedSummaries().isNotEmpty() || db.weekSummaryDao().getDeletedSummaries().isNotEmpty()

        private class PendingChangesException : IllegalStateException()
        private class AccountChangedException : IllegalStateException()
    }

        private fun uriToMultipart(
            context: Context,
            uriString: String,
            uploadKey: String
        ): MultipartBody.Part {

            val uri = Uri.parse(uriString)

            val bytes = context.contentResolver
                .openInputStream(uri)
                ?.use { it.readBytes() }
                ?: throw IllegalArgumentException("사진을 읽을 수 없습니다: $uri")

            val mimeType =
                context.contentResolver.getType(uri)
                    ?: "image/jpeg"

            val requestBody = bytes.toRequestBody(
                mimeType.toMediaTypeOrNull()
            )

            return MultipartBody.Part.createFormData(
                "photos",
                uploadKey,
                requestBody
            )
        }


========== FILE: C:\Users\SAMSUNG\mingu_github\sumdays\Sumdays\app\src\main\java\com\example\sumdays\data\sync\BackupWorker.kt ==========
package com.example.sumdays.data.sync

import android.content.Context
import androidx.work.CoroutineWorker
import androidx.work.WorkerParameters
import androidx.work.workDataOf
import com.example.sumdays.auth.SessionManager

class BackupWorker(
    context: Context,
    params: WorkerParameters
) : CoroutineWorker(context, params) {

    override suspend fun doWork(): Result {
        val expectedUserId = inputData.getInt(InitialSyncWorker.KEY_EXPECTED_USER_ID, -1)
        if (expectedUserId >= 0 && SessionManager.getUserId() != expectedUserId) return Result.failure()
        val syncResult = BackupSyncRunner.syncNow(applicationContext)
        if (syncResult.success) return Result.success()
        if (syncResult.type in setOf("connection_error", "server_retry", "account_sync_pending", "changes_pending")) {
            return Result.retry()
        }
        return Result.failure(
            workDataOf(
                "type" to syncResult.type,
                "message" to syncResult.message,
            )
        )
    }
}


========== FILE: C:\Users\SAMSUNG\mingu_github\sumdays\Sumdays\app\src\main\java\com\example\sumdays\data\sync\InitialSyncRetryPolicy.kt ==========
package com.example.sumdays.data.sync

/** Temporary connectivity/server-load errors must not end initial sync. */
object InitialSyncRetryPolicy {
    fun shouldRetry(status: Int): Boolean = status == 408 || status == 429 || status >= 500
}


========== FILE: C:\Users\SAMSUNG\mingu_github\sumdays\Sumdays\app\src\main\java\com\example\sumdays\data\sync\InitialSyncScheduler.kt ==========
package com.example.sumdays.data.sync

import android.content.Context
import androidx.work.BackoffPolicy
import androidx.work.ExistingWorkPolicy
import androidx.work.OneTimeWorkRequestBuilder
import androidx.work.WorkManager
import androidx.work.workDataOf
import com.example.sumdays.auth.SessionManager
import java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit

/** One recoverable initial-sync job per account. KEEP never cancels an active retry. */
object InitialSyncScheduler {
    fun ensureScheduled(context: Context, replace: Boolean = false) {
        val userId = SessionManager.getUserId()
        if (userId < 0 || LocalDataOwnerPrefs.isReadyFor(context, userId)) return
        val request = OneTimeWorkRequestBuilder<InitialSyncWorker>()
            .setInputData(workDataOf(InitialSyncWorker.KEY_EXPECTED_USER_ID to userId))
            // A reachable LAN/emulator API does not require Android's public
            // internet validation. The worker retries actual connection failures.
            .setBackoffCriteria(BackoffPolicy.EXPONENTIAL, 10, TimeUnit.SECONDS)
            .addTag(InitialSyncWorker.accountWorkTag(userId))
            .build()
        val manager = WorkManager.getInstance(context.applicationContext)
        // Replace jobs installed by the old network-constrained scheduler once.
        manager.cancelUniqueWork("initial_sync_user_$userId")
        manager.enqueueUniqueWork(
            "initial_sync_v2_user_$userId",
            if (replace) ExistingWorkPolicy.REPLACE else ExistingWorkPolicy.KEEP,
            request,
        )
    }
}


========== FILE: C:\Users\SAMSUNG\mingu_github\sumdays\Sumdays\app\src\main\java\com\example\sumdays\data\sync\InitialSyncWorker.kt ==========
package com.example.sumdays.data.sync

// --- 너의 엔티티 & DAO 관련 ---

// --- 네트워크 ---
import android.content.Context
import android.util.Log
import androidx.room.withTransaction
import androidx.work.CoroutineWorker
import androidx.work.WorkerParameters
import androidx.work.workDataOf
import com.example.sumdays.auth.SessionManager
import com.example.sumdays.data.AppDatabase
import com.example.sumdays.data.DailyEntry
import com.example.sumdays.data.EmotionAnalysis
import com.example.sumdays.data.Highlight
import com.example.sumdays.data.Insights
import com.example.sumdays.data.Memo
import com.example.sumdays.data.SummaryDetails
import com.example.sumdays.data.UserStyle
import com.example.sumdays.data.WeekSummary
import com.example.sumdays.data.WeekSummaryEntity
import com.example.sumdays.data.style.StylePrompt
import com.example.sumdays.mission.MissionConfig
import com.example.sumdays.network.ApiClient
import com.example.sumdays.settings.prefs.ProfileImagePrefs
import com.example.sumdays.shop.PointWalletRepository
import com.google.gson.Gson
import kotlinx.coroutines.CancellationException
import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
import kotlinx.coroutines.withContext
import java.io.IOException

class InitialSyncWorker(
    context: Context,
    params: WorkerParameters,
) : CoroutineWorker(context, params) {

    private fun convertToWeekSummary(p: WeekSummaryPayload, gson: Gson): WeekSummary {
        val emotionAnalysis = gson.fromJson(
            gson.toJson(p.emotionAnalysis),
            EmotionAnalysis::class.java
        )

        val highlights = gson.fromJson(
            gson.toJson(p.highlights),
            Array<Highlight>::class.java
        ).toList()

        val insights = gson.fromJson(
            gson.toJson(p.insights),
            Insights::class.java
        )

        val summary = gson.fromJson(
            gson.toJson(p.summary),
            SummaryDetails::class.java
        )

        return WeekSummary(
            startDate = p.startDate,
            endDate = p.endDate,
            diaryCount = p.diaryCount ?: 0,
            emotionAnalysis = emotionAnalysis,
            highlights = highlights,
            insights = insights,
            summary = summary
        )
    }


    override suspend fun doWork(): Result = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {

        try {
            Log.d("initWork","dowork (back to frontr")

            val expectedUserId = inputData.getInt(KEY_EXPECTED_USER_ID, -1)
            if (expectedUserId < 0 || SessionManager.getUserId() != expectedUserId) {
                return@withContext Result.failure()
            }

            // 0) login 되어있는지 확인
            val token = SessionManager.getToken()
            if(token == null) {
                val serverFailData = workDataOf(
                    "type" to "token_error",
                    "message" to "token이 유효하지 않음"
                )
                return@withContext Result.failure(serverFailData)
            }
            val tokenHeader = "Bearer ${token}"

            // 1) 서버에서 전체 데이터 가져오기
            val response = ApiClient.api.fetchServerData(tokenHeader)
            if (!response.isSuccessful) {
                return@withContext if (InitialSyncRetryPolicy.shouldRetry(response.code())) {
                    Result.retry()
                } else {
                    Result.failure(workDataOf("message" to "초기 동기화 거절: ${response.code()}"))
                }
            }
            if (response.body() == null) return@withContext Result.failure()
            val serverData = response.body()!!

            if (SessionManager.getUserId() != expectedUserId) {
                return@withContext Result.failure()
            }


            /// printEditedUserStyles(frp = serverData)

            // 2) Room DB 준비
            val db = AppDatabase.getDatabase(applicationContext)
            val memoDao = db.memoDao()
            val dailyDao = db.dailyEntryDao()
            val styleDao = db.userStyleDao()
            val weekDao = db.weekSummaryDao()

            // 3) 삭제와 삽입을 한 트랜잭션으로 처리해 중간 상태를 노출하지 않습니다.
            db.withTransaction {
                if (SessionManager.getUserId() != expectedUserId) {
                    throw AccountChangedException()
                }

                memoDao.clearAll()
                dailyDao.clearAll()
                styleDao.clearAll()
                weekDao.clearAll()

                // --- 서버 데이터 삽입 ---
                // Memo
                val memoList = serverData.memo.map { payload ->
                    Memo(
                        id = payload.room_id,
                        content = payload.content ?: "",
                        timestamp = payload.timestamp ?: "",
                        date = payload.date,
                        order = payload.memo_order,
                        type = payload.type ?: "text",
                        isEdited = false,
                        isDeleted = false
                    )
                }
                memoDao.insertAll(memoList)

                // DailyEntry
                val dailyEntryList = serverData.dailyEntry.map { p ->
                    DailyEntry(
                        date = p.date,
                        diary = p.diary,
                        keywords = p.keywords,
                        aiComment = p.aiComment,
                        emotionScore = p.emotionScore,
                        emotionIcon = p.emotionIcon,
                        themeIcon = p.themeIcon,
                        photoUrls = p.photoUrls,
                        isAllowed = (p.is_allowed ?: 0) != 0,
                        isEdited = false,
                        isDeleted = false
                    )
                }

                dailyEntryList.forEach { entry ->
                    Log.d("Sync-DailyEntry", """
        ---- DailyEntry ----
        date = ${entry.date}
        diary = ${entry.diary}
        keywords = ${entry.keywords}
        aiComment = ${entry.aiComment}
        emotionScore = ${entry.emotionScore}
        emotionIcon = ${entry.emotionIcon}
        themeIcon = ${entry.themeIcon}
        photoUrls = ${entry.photoUrls}
        isAllowed = ${entry.isAllowed}
        isEdited = ${entry.isEdited}
        isDeleted = ${entry.isDeleted}
    """.trimIndent())
                }

                dailyDao.insertAll(dailyEntryList)

                // UserStyle
                val gson = Gson()
                val styleList = serverData.userStyle.map { p ->
                    val stylePrompt = gson.fromJson(
                        gson.toJson(p.stylePrompt),
                        StylePrompt::class.java
                    )
                    UserStyle(
                        styleId = p.styleId,
                        styleName = p.styleName,
                        styleVector = p.styleVector,
                        styleExamples = p.styleExamples,
                        stylePrompt = stylePrompt,
                        sampleDiary = p.sampleDiary,
                        isEdited = false,
                        isDeleted = false
                    )
                }
                styleDao.insertAll(styleList)

                // WeekSummary
                val weekSummaryList = serverData.weekSummary.map { payload ->
                    WeekSummaryEntity(
                        startDate = payload.startDate,
                        weekSummary = convertToWeekSummary(payload, gson),
                        isEdited = false,
                        isDeleted = false
                    )
                }
                weekDao.insertAll(weekSummaryList)

                if (SessionManager.getUserId() != expectedUserId) {
                    throw AccountChangedException()
                }
            }

            if (SessionManager.getUserId() != expectedUserId) {
                return@withContext Result.failure()
            }

            // 4) pref 초기화
            // TODO : 다른 pref 초기화
            // 4) 프로필 초기 동기화
            ProfileImagePrefs.clear(applicationContext)
            val profile = serverData.profile

            if (profile != null) {
                when (profile.mode) {
                    "AVATAR" -> {
                        ProfileImagePrefs.setProfileIds(
                            context = applicationContext,
                            face = profile.faceId ?: -1,
                            eyes = profile.eyesId ?: -1,
                            mouth = profile.mouthId ?: -1,
                            acc = profile.accessoryId ?: -1
                        )
                        ProfileImagePrefs.setMode(applicationContext, "AVATAR")
                    }

                    "PHOTO" -> {
                        ProfileImagePrefs.setImageUrl(
                            applicationContext,
                            profile.imageUrl
                        )
                        ProfileImagePrefs.setMode(applicationContext, "PHOTO")
                    }

                    else -> {
                        Log.w("InitialSyncWorker", "Unknown profile mode: ${profile.mode}")
                    }
                }
            }

            Log.d(
                "ProfileDebug",
                "mode=${ProfileImagePrefs.getMode(applicationContext)}, " +
                        "imageUrl=${ProfileImagePrefs.getImageUrl(applicationContext)}"
            )

            if (MissionConfig.USE_SERVER_MISSIONS) {
                runCatching {
                    PointWalletRepository(applicationContext).refreshShopCatalog()
                }.onFailure { error ->
                    if (error is CancellationException) throw error
                    Log.w("InitialSyncWorker", "상점 인벤토리 초기 동기화 실패", error)
                }
            }

            if (SessionManager.getUserId() != expectedUserId) {
                return@withContext Result.failure()
            }
            LocalDataOwnerPrefs.markReady(applicationContext, expectedUserId)

            return@withContext Result.success()

        } catch (e: CancellationException) {
            throw e
        } catch (e: IOException) {
            // Keep ownership unready; WorkManager resumes after connectivity recovers.
            Log.w("InitialSyncWorker", "초기 동기화 연결 실패: 자동 재시도", e)
            return@withContext Result.retry()
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            e.printStackTrace()
            return@withContext Result.failure()
        }
    }

    companion object {
        const val KEY_EXPECTED_USER_ID = "expected_user_id"

        fun accountWorkTag(userId: Int): String = "account_work_user_$userId"
    }

    private class AccountChangedException : IllegalStateException()
}


========== FILE: C:\Users\SAMSUNG\mingu_github\sumdays\Sumdays\app\src\main\java\com\example\sumdays\data\sync\LocalDataOwnerPrefs.kt ==========
package com.example.sumdays.data.sync

import android.content.Context
import com.example.sumdays.auth.SessionManager

/**
 * 계정 공용 Room DB가 현재 어느 사용자의 서버 데이터와 동기화됐는지 기록합니다.
 * 소유자가 일치하지 않는 동안에는 로컬 데이터를 서버로 업로드하지 않습니다.
 */
object LocalDataOwnerPrefs {
    private const val PREF_NAME = "local_data_owner"
    private const val KEY_READY_USER_ID = "ready_user_id"
    private const val NO_USER = -1

    fun prepareForCurrentSession(context: Context) {
        val userId = currentUserId()
        if (userId < 0) return

        val prefs = prefs(context)
        if (!prefs.contains(KEY_READY_USER_ID)) {
            prefs.edit().putInt(KEY_READY_USER_ID, userId).apply()
        }
    }

    fun beginAccountSwitch(context: Context) {
        prefs(context).edit().putInt(KEY_READY_USER_ID, NO_USER).apply()
    }

    fun markReady(context: Context, userId: Int) {
        if (currentUserId() == userId) {
            prefs(context).edit().putInt(KEY_READY_USER_ID, userId).apply()
        }
    }

    fun isReadyFor(context: Context, userId: Int): Boolean =
        userId >= 0 && prefs(context).getInt(KEY_READY_USER_ID, NO_USER) == userId

    fun clear(context: Context) {
        prefs(context).edit().putInt(KEY_READY_USER_ID, NO_USER).apply()
    }

    private fun prefs(context: Context) =
        context.getSharedPreferences(PREF_NAME, Context.MODE_PRIVATE)

    private fun currentUserId(): Int =
        runCatching { SessionManager.getUserId() }.getOrDefault(NO_USER)
}


========== FILE: C:\Users\SAMSUNG\mingu_github\sumdays\Sumdays\app\src\main\java\com\example\sumdays\data\sync\SyncDto.kt ==========
package com.example.sumdays.data.sync

import com.example.sumdays.data.DailyEntry
import com.example.sumdays.data.Memo
import com.example.sumdays.data.UserStyle
import com.example.sumdays.data.WeekSummary
import android.os.Parcelable
import kotlinx.parcelize.Parcelize

// ------------------ 최상위 Request ------------------
data class SyncRequest(
    val deleted: DeletedPayload?,
    val edited: EditedPayload?
)


data class SyncFetchResponse(
    val memo: List<MemoPayload>,
    val dailyEntry: List<DailyEntryPayload>,
    val weekSummary: List<WeekSummaryPayload>,
    val userStyle: List<UserStylePayload>,
    val profile: ProfilePayload? = null
)
@Parcelize
data class ProfilePayload(
    val mode: String, // "AVATAR" or "PHOTO"
    val faceId: Int? = null,
    val eyesId: Int? = null,
    val mouthId: Int? = null,
    val accessoryId: Int? = null,
    val imageUrl: String? = null
): Parcelable
// ------------------ 삭제 데이터 (ID 리스트만) ------------------
data class DeletedPayload(
    val memo: List<Int>?,
    val userStyle: List<Long>?,
    val dailyEntry: List<String>?,
    val weekSummary: List<String>?
)

// ------------------ 수정/생성 데이터 (객체 리스트) ------------------
data class EditedPayload(
    val memo: List<MemoPayload>?,
    val userStyle: List<UserStylePayload>?,
    val dailyEntry: List<DailyEntryPayload>?,
    val dailyPhotos: List<DailyPhotosPayload>?,
    val weekSummary: List<WeekSummaryPayload>?
)

// ------------------ Memo 데이터 ------------------
data class MemoPayload(
    val room_id: Int,
    val content: String?,
    val timestamp: String?,
    val date: String,
    val memo_order: Int,
    val type: String?
)

// ------------------ DailyEntry 데이터 ------------------
data class DailyEntryPayload(
    val date: String,
    val diary: String?,
    val keywords: String?,
    val aiComment: String?,
    val emotionScore: Double?,
    val emotionIcon: String?,
    val themeIcon: String?,
    val photoUrls: String?,
    val is_allowed: Int?
)

// ------------------ DailyPhotos 데이터 ------------------
data class DailyPhotosPayload(
    val date: String,
    val serverPhotoUrls: String?,
    val uploadKeys: List<String>?
)
// ------------------ WeekSummary 데이터 ------------------
data class WeekSummaryPayload(
    val startDate: String,
    val endDate: String,
    val diaryCount: Int,
    val emotionAnalysis: Any,
    val highlights: Any,
    val insights: Any,
    val summary: Any
)

// ------------------ UserStyle 데이터 ------------------
data class UserStylePayload(
    val styleId: Long,
    val styleName: String,
    val styleVector: List<Float>,
    val styleExamples: List<String>,
    val stylePrompt: Any, // Gson이 JSON → Any 로 자동 매핑
    val sampleDiary: String
)

// ------------------ 서버 응답 ------------------
data class SyncResponse(
    val status: String,
    val message: String,
    val dailyPhotos: List<SyncedDailyPhotoUrlsPayload>?
)
data class SyncedDailyPhotoUrlsPayload(
    val date: String,
    val photoUrls: String
)



// request data 형태로 보내주는 함수
fun buildSyncRequest(
    deletedMemoIds: List<Int>,
    deletedStyleIds: List<Long>,
    deletedEntryDates: List<String>,
    deletedSummaryStartDates: List<String>,

    editedMemos: List<Memo>,                    // ← Room Memo
    editedStyles: List<UserStyle>,              // ← Room UserStyle
    editedEntries: List<DailyEntry>,            // ← Room DailyEntry
    editedSummaries: List<WeekSummary>,          // ← Room WeekSummary
    dailyPhotosPayloads: List<DailyPhotosPayload>
): SyncRequest {

    val deletedPayload = DeletedPayload(
        memo = deletedMemoIds.ifEmpty { null },
        userStyle = deletedStyleIds.ifEmpty { null },
        dailyEntry = deletedEntryDates.ifEmpty { null },
        weekSummary = deletedSummaryStartDates.ifEmpty { null }
    )

    // ----- Room → Payload 변환 -----
    val memoPayloads = editedMemos.map {
        MemoPayload(
            room_id = it.id,
            date = it.date,
            memo_order = it.order,
            content = it.content,
            timestamp = it.timestamp,
            type = it.type
        )
    }

    val stylePayloads = editedStyles.map {
        UserStylePayload(
            styleId = it.styleId.toLong(),
            styleName = it.styleName,
            styleVector = it.styleVector,
            styleExamples = it.styleExamples,
            stylePrompt = it.stylePrompt,
            sampleDiary = it.sampleDiary
        )
    }

    val entryPayloads = editedEntries.map {
        DailyEntryPayload(
            date = it.date,
            diary = it.diary,
            keywords = it.keywords,
            aiComment = it.aiComment,
            emotionScore = it.emotionScore,
            emotionIcon = it.emotionIcon,
            themeIcon = it.themeIcon,
            photoUrls = "", // dailyPhotosPayloads에서 따로 처리
            is_allowed = if (it.isAllowed == true) 1 else 0,
        )
    }

    val summaryPayloads = editedSummaries.map {
        WeekSummaryPayload(
            startDate = it.startDate,
            endDate = it.endDate,
            diaryCount = it.diaryCount,
            emotionAnalysis = it.emotionAnalysis,
            highlights = it.highlights,
            insights = it.insights,
            summary = it.summary
        )
    }

    val editedPayload = EditedPayload(
        memo = memoPayloads.ifEmpty { null },
        userStyle = stylePayloads.ifEmpty { null },
        dailyEntry = entryPayloads.ifEmpty { null },
        dailyPhotos = dailyPhotosPayloads.ifEmpty { null },
        weekSummary = summaryPayloads.ifEmpty { null }
    )

    // 둘 다 null이면 서버에 보낼 필요 없음
    return SyncRequest(
        deleted = if (
            deletedMemoIds.isEmpty() &&
            deletedStyleIds.isEmpty() &&
            deletedEntryDates.isEmpty() &&
            deletedSummaryStartDates.isEmpty()
        ) null else deletedPayload,

        edited = if (
            editedMemos.isEmpty() &&
            editedStyles.isEmpty() &&
            editedEntries.isEmpty() &&
            editedSummaries.isEmpty() &&
            dailyPhotosPayloads.isEmpty()
        ) null else editedPayload
    )
}


========== FILE: C:\Users\SAMSUNG\mingu_github\sumdays\Sumdays\app\src\main\java\com\example\sumdays\image\PhotoGalleryAdapter.kt ==========
package com.example.sumdays.image

import android.net.Uri
import android.util.Base64
import android.view.LayoutInflater
import android.view.View
import android.view.ViewGroup
import android.widget.ImageView
import androidx.cardview.widget.CardView
import androidx.core.content.ContextCompat
import androidx.recyclerview.widget.ListAdapter
import androidx.recyclerview.widget.RecyclerView
import com.bumptech.glide.Glide
import com.example.sumdays.R
import com.example.sumdays.theme.ThemePrefs
import com.example.sumdays.theme.ThemeRepository

class PhotoGalleryAdapter(
    private val onPhotoClick: (String) -> Unit,
    private val onDeleteClick: (Int) -> Unit,
    private val onAddClick: () -> Unit,
    private val isReadOnly: Boolean = false
) : ListAdapter<GalleryItem, RecyclerView.ViewHolder>(GalleryDiffCallback()) {

    companion object {
        private const val VIEW_TYPE_PHOTO = 1
        private const val VIEW_TYPE_ADD = 2
    }

    override fun getItemViewType(position: Int): Int =
        when (getItem(position)) {
            is GalleryItem.Photo -> VIEW_TYPE_PHOTO
            is GalleryItem.Add -> VIEW_TYPE_ADD
        }

    override fun onCreateViewHolder(parent: ViewGroup, viewType: Int): RecyclerView.ViewHolder {
        val inflater = LayoutInflater.from(parent.context)
        return when (viewType) {
            VIEW_TYPE_PHOTO -> {
                val view = inflater.inflate(R.layout.item_photo_gallery, parent, false)
                PhotoViewHolder(view)
            }
            VIEW_TYPE_ADD -> {
                val view = inflater.inflate(R.layout.item_photo_gallery_add, parent, false)
                AddViewHolder(view)
            }
            else -> throw IllegalArgumentException("Invalid view type: $viewType")
        }
    }

    override fun onBindViewHolder(holder: RecyclerView.ViewHolder, position: Int) {
        when (val item = getItem(position)) {
            is GalleryItem.Photo -> {
                (holder as PhotoViewHolder).bind(
                    url = item.url,
                    position = position,
                    onClick = onPhotoClick,
                    onDeleteClick = onDeleteClick,
                    isReadOnly = isReadOnly
                )
            }
            is GalleryItem.Add -> {
                (holder as AddViewHolder).bind(onAddClick)
            }
        }
    }

    class PhotoViewHolder(itemView: View) : RecyclerView.ViewHolder(itemView) {
        private val cardView: CardView = itemView as CardView
        private val imageView: ImageView = itemView.findViewById(R.id.gallery_image)
        private val deleteButton: ImageView = itemView.findViewById(R.id.btn_delete_image)

        fun bind(
            url: String,
            position: Int,
            onClick: (String) -> Unit,
            onDeleteClick: (Int) -> Unit,
            isReadOnly: Boolean

        ) {
            applyTheme()

            val isUriOrPath = url.startsWith("content://") ||
                    url.startsWith("file://") ||
                    url.startsWith("http://") ||
                    url.startsWith("https://") ||
                    url.startsWith("/")

            if (isUriOrPath) {
                Glide.with(itemView.context)
                    .load(Uri.parse(url))
                    .centerCrop()
                    .error(android.R.drawable.ic_menu_report_image)
                    .into(imageView)
            } else {
                try {
                    val imageBytes = Base64.decode(url, Base64.DEFAULT)
                    Glide.with(itemView.context)
                        .load(imageBytes)
                        .centerCrop()
                        .error(android.R.drawable.ic_menu_report_image)
                        .into(imageView)
                } catch (_: IllegalArgumentException) {
                    Glide.with(itemView.context)
                        .load(url)
                        .centerCrop()
                        .error(android.R.drawable.ic_menu_report_image)
                        .into(imageView)
                }
            }

            imageView.setOnClickListener { onClick(url) }
            deleteButton.visibility = if (isReadOnly) View.GONE else View.VISIBLE
            deleteButton.setOnClickListener { onDeleteClick(position) }
        }

        private fun applyTheme() {
            val context = itemView.context
            val themeKey = ThemePrefs.getTheme(context)
            val currentTheme = ThemeRepository.ownedThemes[themeKey] ?: return

            val blockColor = ContextCompat.getColor(context, currentTheme.themeColorA)
            val primaryColor = ContextCompat.getColor(context, currentTheme.themeTextColorSpecialA)

            cardView.setCardBackgroundColor(blockColor)
            deleteButton.setColorFilter(primaryColor)
        }
    }

    class AddViewHolder(itemView: View) : RecyclerView.ViewHolder(itemView) {
        private val cardView: CardView = itemView as CardView
        private val addIcon: ImageView = itemView.findViewById(R.id.add_icon)

        fun bind(onClick: () -> Unit) {
            applyTheme()
            itemView.setOnClickListener { onClick() }
        }

        private fun applyTheme() {
            val context = itemView.context
            val themeKey = ThemePrefs.getTheme(context)
            val currentTheme = ThemeRepository.ownedThemes[themeKey] ?: return

            val blockColor = ContextCompat.getColor(context, currentTheme.themeColorA)
            val primaryColor = ContextCompat.getColor(context, currentTheme.themeTextColorSpecialA)

            cardView.setCardBackgroundColor(blockColor)
            addIcon.setColorFilter(primaryColor)
        }
    }
}


========== FILE: C:\Users\SAMSUNG\mingu_github\sumdays\Sumdays\app\src\main\java\com\example\sumdays\image\ProfileRenderer.kt ==========

package com.example.sumdays.image

import android.graphics.Color
import android.view.View
import android.widget.ImageView
import androidx.annotation.DrawableRes
import com.bumptech.glide.Glide
import com.bumptech.glide.load.engine.GlideException
import com.example.sumdays.R
import com.example.sumdays.data.sync.ProfilePayload
import com.example.sumdays.network.ApiClient
import com.example.sumdays.settings.prefs.ProfileImagePrefs
import com.example.sumdays.settings.profileimage.ProfileImageItem
import com.example.sumdays.settings.profileimage.ProfileImageItemType
import android.graphics.drawable.Drawable
import com.bumptech.glide.load.DataSource
import com.bumptech.glide.request.RequestListener
import com.bumptech.glide.request.target.Target

object ProfileRenderer {

    fun render(
        root: View,
        profile: ProfilePayload?,
        isMine : Boolean
    ) {
        val photo = root.findViewById<ImageView>(R.id.profilePhoto)
        val base = root.findViewById<ImageView>(R.id.profileBase)
        val mouth = root.findViewById<ImageView>(R.id.profileMouth)
        val eyes = root.findViewById<ImageView>(R.id.profileEyes)
        val accessory = root.findViewById<ImageView>(R.id.profileAccessory)

        val avatarViews = listOf(base, mouth, eyes, accessory)


        // 이전 렌더링 상태 초기화
        Glide.with(root).clear(photo)
        photo.setImageDrawable(null)
        photo.visibility = View.GONE

        avatarViews.forEach {
            it.setImageDrawable(null)
            it.clearColorFilter()
            it.visibility = View.GONE
        }

        // 기본 이미지 표시
        fun showDefault() {
            photo.visibility = View.GONE

            avatarViews.forEach {
                it.visibility = View.GONE
            }

            if (isMine) {
                base.setImageResource(R.drawable.nav_fox_button)
                base.visibility = View.VISIBLE
            } else {
                photo.setImageResource(R.drawable.ic_account_circle)
                photo.visibility = View.VISIBLE
            }
        }

        if (profile == null) {
            showDefault()
            return
        }

        when (profile.mode) {

            "PHOTO" -> {
                val imageUrl = ProfileImagePrefs.parseImageUrl(profile.imageUrl)

                if (imageUrl == null) {
                    showDefault()
                    return
                }

                photo.visibility = View.VISIBLE

                Glide.with(root)
                    .load(imageUrl)
                    .listener(object : RequestListener<Drawable> {
                        override fun onLoadFailed(
                            e: GlideException?,
                            model: Any?,
                            target: Target<Drawable>,
                            isFirstResource: Boolean
                        ): Boolean {
                            showDefault()
                            return true
                        }

                        override fun onResourceReady(
                            resource: Drawable,
                            model: Any,
                            target: Target<Drawable>?,
                            dataSource: DataSource,
                            isFirstResource: Boolean
                        ): Boolean = false
                    })
                    .into(photo)
            }

            "AVATAR" -> {
                val isAvatarEmpty =
                    profile.faceId == -1 &&
                            profile.eyesId == -1 &&
                            profile.mouthId == -1 &&
                            profile.accessoryId == -1

                if (isAvatarEmpty) {
                    showDefault()
                    return
                }

                // 기존 SocialActivity.kt의 임시 리소스 목록
                val items = listOf(
                    ProfileImageItem(1, ProfileImageItemType.FACE, R.drawable.nav_fox_button),
                    ProfileImageItem(2, ProfileImageItemType.FACE, R.drawable.dailyread_fox_face_level_5),
                    ProfileImageItem(3, ProfileImageItemType.FACE, 0),
                    ProfileImageItem(4, ProfileImageItemType.EYES, R.drawable.loading_animation),
                    ProfileImageItem(5, ProfileImageItemType.EYES, 0)
                )

                fun renderPart(
                    view: ImageView,
                    type: ProfileImageItemType,
                    id: Int?,
                    color: Int
                ) {
                    if (id == null || id == -1) return

                    val resId = items.find {
                        it.type == type && it.id == id
                    }?.resId ?: return

                    if (resId == 0) return

                    view.setImageResource(resId)
                    view.setColorFilter(color)
                    view.visibility = View.VISIBLE
                }

                // 얼굴: 설정되지 않았으면 기본 여우 얼굴 표시
                val faceResId = items.find {
                    it.type == ProfileImageItemType.FACE &&
                            it.id == profile.faceId
                }?.resId?.takeIf { it != 0 } ?: R.drawable.nav_fox_button

                base.setImageResource(faceResId)
                base.setColorFilter(Color.rgb(255, 224, 189))
                base.visibility = View.VISIBLE

                // 나머지 파츠
                renderPart(
                    mouth,
                    ProfileImageItemType.MOUTH,
                    profile.mouthId,
                    Color.CYAN
                )

                renderPart(
                    eyes,
                    ProfileImageItemType.EYES,
                    profile.eyesId,
                    Color.BLACK
                )

                renderPart(
                    accessory,
                    ProfileImageItemType.ACC,
                    profile.accessoryId,
                    Color.YELLOW
                )
            }

            else -> showDefault()
        }
    }
}


========== FILE: C:\Users\SAMSUNG\mingu_github\sumdays\Sumdays\app\src\main\java\com\example\sumdays\image\ProfileUploadHelper.kt ==========
package com.example.sumdays.image
import android.content.Context
import com.example.sumdays.auth.SessionManager
import com.example.sumdays.network.ApiClient
import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
import kotlinx.coroutines.withContext
import okhttp3.MediaType.Companion.toMediaTypeOrNull
import okhttp3.MultipartBody
import okhttp3.RequestBody.Companion.asRequestBody
import java.io.File
import com.example.sumdays.network.apiService.UpdateProfileAvatarRequest
import com.example.sumdays.settings.prefs.ProfileImagePrefs

suspend fun uploadProfileImageToServer(
    context: Context,
    imageFile: File
): Boolean = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
    try {
        val rawToken = SessionManager.getToken()
            ?: return@withContext false

        val token = if (rawToken.startsWith("Bearer ")) {
            rawToken
        } else {
            "Bearer $rawToken"
        }

        val requestBody = imageFile.asRequestBody(
            "image/*".toMediaTypeOrNull()
        )

        val part = MultipartBody.Part.createFormData(
            "profileImage",
            imageFile.name,
            requestBody
        )

        val response = ApiClient.api.updateProfileImage(token, part)

        if (response.isSuccessful && response.body()?.success == true) {
            val imageUrl = response.body()?.profileImageUrl
                ?: return@withContext false

            ProfileImagePrefs.setImageUrl(context, imageUrl)
            ProfileImagePrefs.setMode(context, "PHOTO")

            true
        } else {
            false
        }
    } catch (e: Exception) {
        e.printStackTrace()
        false
    }
}

/**
 * 아바타 설정을 서버에 저장
 * @return 저장 성공 여부
 */
suspend fun uploadProfileAvatarToServer(
    faceId: Int,
    eyesId: Int,
    mouthId: Int,
    accessoryId: Int
): Boolean = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
    try {
        val rawToken = SessionManager.getToken()
            ?: return@withContext false

        val token = if (rawToken.startsWith("Bearer ")) {
            rawToken
        } else {
            "Bearer $rawToken"
        }

        val request = UpdateProfileAvatarRequest(
            faceId = faceId,
            eyesId = eyesId,
            mouthId = mouthId,
            accessoryId = accessoryId
        )

        val response = ApiClient.api.updateProfileAvatar(token, request)

        response.isSuccessful && response.body()?.success == true

    } catch (e: Exception) {
        e.printStackTrace()
        false
    }
}


========== FILE: C:\Users\SAMSUNG\mingu_github\sumdays\Sumdays\app\src\main\java\com\example\sumdays\mission\MissionEventWorker.kt ==========
package com.example.sumdays.mission

import android.content.Context
import androidx.work.CoroutineWorker
import androidx.work.WorkerParameters
import com.example.sumdays.auth.SessionManager
import com.example.sumdays.data.sync.BackupSyncRunner
import com.example.sumdays.network.ApiClient

class MissionEventWorker(
    context: Context,
    params: WorkerParameters,
) : CoroutineWorker(context, params) {

    override suspend fun doWork(): Result {
        val expectedUserId = inputData.getInt(KEY_EXPECTED_USER_ID, -1)
        if (expectedUserId < 0 || SessionManager.getUserId() != expectedUserId) {
            return Result.failure()
        }
        val token = SessionManager.getToken() ?: return Result.failure()
        val tokenHeader = "Bearer $token"

        val response = try {
            when (inputData.getString(KEY_EVENT_TYPE)) {
                EVENT_FRIEND_DIARY -> {
                    val friendId = inputData.getInt(KEY_FRIEND_ID, -1)
                    val diaryDate = inputData.getString(KEY_EVENT_KEY) ?: return Result.failure()
                    if (friendId < 0) return Result.failure()
                    ApiClient.api.recordFriendDiaryView(
                        tokenHeader,
                        FriendDiaryViewRequest(friendId, diaryDate),
                    )
                }
                EVENT_WEEKLY_SUMMARY -> {
                    val summaryStartDate = inputData.getString(KEY_EVENT_KEY)
                        ?: return Result.failure()
                    val sync = BackupSyncRunner.syncNow(applicationContext)
                    if (!sync.success) return Result.retry()
                    ApiClient.api.recordWeeklySummaryView(
                        tokenHeader,
                        WeeklySummaryViewRequest(summaryStartDate)
                    )
                }
                else -> return Result.failure()
            }
        } catch (_: Exception) {
            return Result.retry()
        }

        return when {
            response.isSuccessful -> Result.success()
            response.code() == 408 || response.code() == 429 || response.code() >= 500 -> Result.retry()
            else -> Result.failure()
        }
    }

    companion object {
        const val KEY_EVENT_TYPE = "event_type"
        const val KEY_EVENT_KEY = "event_key"
        const val KEY_FRIEND_ID = "friend_id"
        const val KEY_EXPECTED_USER_ID = "expected_user_id"
        const val EVENT_FRIEND_DIARY = "friend_diary"
        const val EVENT_WEEKLY_SUMMARY = "weekly_summary"
    }
}


========== FILE: C:\Users\SAMSUNG\mingu_github\sumdays\Sumdays\app\src\main\java\com\example\sumdays\mission\MissionRepository.kt ==========
package com.example.sumdays.mission

import android.content.Context
import com.example.sumdays.auth.SessionManager
import com.example.sumdays.data.AppDatabase
import com.example.sumdays.shop.PointPrefs
import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
import kotlinx.coroutines.sync.Mutex
import kotlinx.coroutines.sync.withLock
import kotlinx.coroutines.withContext
import org.threeten.bp.DayOfWeek
import org.threeten.bp.LocalDate
import org.threeten.bp.ZoneId

interface MissionRepository {
    suspend fun getMissions(): List<Mission>
    suspend fun claimReward(missionCode: String): Int?
    fun getAdRewardState(): AdRewardState
    suspend fun claimAdReward(rewardId: String): AdRewardResult
}

class LocalMissionRepository(
    context: Context,
    private val database: AppDatabase = AppDatabase.getDatabase(context),
) : MissionRepository {
    private val appContext = context.applicationContext
    private val claimMutex = Mutex()

    override suspend fun getMissions(): List<Mission> {
        val today = koreaToday()
        val weekStart = today.with(DayOfWeek.MONDAY)
        val weekEnd = weekStart.plusDays(6)

        val roomProgress = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
            val memoCount = database.memoDao().getMemoCountByDate(today.toString())
            val todayEntry = database.dailyEntryDao().getEntrySnapshot(today.toString())
            val weeklyWrittenDates = database.dailyEntryDao()
                .getEntriesBetween(weekStart.toString(), weekEnd.toString())
                .asSequence()
                .filter { !it.isDeleted && !it.diary.isNullOrBlank() }
                .map { LocalDate.parse(it.date) }
                .distinct()
                .sorted()
                .toList()

            RoomMissionProgress(
                memoCount = memoCount,
                isTodayDiaryComplete = !todayEntry?.diary.isNullOrBlank() && todayEntry?.isDeleted == false,
                weeklyDiaryCount = weeklyWrittenDates.size,
                weeklyStreak = longestConsecutiveDays(weeklyWrittenDates),
            )
        }
        val summaryRewardState = MissionEventPrefs.getWeeklySummaryRewardState(appContext)

        val progressByCode = mapOf(
            MissionCode.DAILY_WRITE_MEMO to roomProgress.memoCount,
            MissionCode.DAILY_COMPLETE_DIARY to if (roomProgress.isTodayDiaryComplete) 1 else 0,
            MissionCode.DAILY_VIEW_FRIEND_DIARY to if (
                MissionEventPrefs.hasFriendDiaryView(appContext, today)
            ) 1 else 0,
            MissionCode.WEEKLY_DIARY_STREAK to roomProgress.weeklyStreak,
            MissionCode.WEEKLY_WRITE_DIARY to roomProgress.weeklyDiaryCount,
            MissionCode.WEEKLY_SUMMARY_REWARD to if (summaryRewardState.readySummaryId != null) 1 else 0,
        )

        return MissionCatalog.definitions.map { definition ->
            val progress = progressByCode[definition.code]
                ?.coerceIn(0, definition.targetCount)
                ?: 0
            val status = if (definition.code == MissionCode.WEEKLY_SUMMARY_REWARD) {
                when {
                    summaryRewardState.readySummaryId != null -> MissionStatus.CLAIMABLE
                    summaryRewardState.latestSummaryRewarded -> MissionStatus.CLAIMED
                    else -> MissionStatus.IN_PROGRESS
                }
            } else {
                when {
                    MissionStatePrefs.isClaimed(appContext, definition, today, weekStart) ->
                        MissionStatus.CLAIMED
                    progress >= definition.targetCount -> MissionStatus.CLAIMABLE
                    else -> MissionStatus.IN_PROGRESS
                }
            }

            Mission(definition, progress, status)
        }
    }

    override suspend fun claimReward(missionCode: String): Int? = claimMutex.withLock {
        val mission = getMissions().firstOrNull {
            it.definition.code == missionCode
        } ?: return@withLock null

        if (mission.status != MissionStatus.CLAIMABLE) return@withLock null

        if (missionCode == MissionCode.WEEKLY_SUMMARY_REWARD) {
            val summaryId = MissionEventPrefs.getWeeklySummaryRewardState(appContext)
                .readySummaryId ?: return@withLock null
            MissionEventPrefs.markWeeklySummaryRewarded(appContext, summaryId)
        } else {
            val today = koreaToday()
            val weekStart = today.with(DayOfWeek.MONDAY)
            MissionStatePrefs.markClaimed(appContext, mission.definition, today, weekStart)
        }

        val currentPoint = PointPrefs.getPoint(appContext)
        PointPrefs.savePoint(appContext, currentPoint + mission.definition.rewardPoints)
        mission.definition.rewardPoints
    }

    override fun getAdRewardState(): AdRewardState = AdRewardState(
        watchedCount = MissionEventPrefs.getAdViewCount(appContext, koreaToday()),
        dailyLimit = MissionConfig.AD_DAILY_LIMIT,
        rewardPoints = MissionConfig.AD_REWARD,
    )

    override suspend fun claimAdReward(rewardId: String): AdRewardResult = claimMutex.withLock {
        val state = getAdRewardState()
        if (MissionEventPrefs.isAdRewardProcessed(appContext, rewardId)) {
            return@withLock AdRewardResult.Granted(
                rewardPoints = state.rewardPoints,
                watchedCount = state.watchedCount,
                dailyLimit = state.dailyLimit,
            )
        }
        if (!state.canWatch) return@withLock AdRewardResult.DailyLimitReached

        val watchedCount = MissionEventPrefs.incrementAdViewCount(appContext, koreaToday())
        MissionEventPrefs.markAdRewardProcessed(appContext, rewardId)
        val currentPoint = PointPrefs.getPoint(appContext)
        PointPrefs.savePoint(appContext, currentPoint + state.rewardPoints)

        AdRewardResult.Granted(
            rewardPoints = state.rewardPoints,
            watchedCount = watchedCount,
            dailyLimit = state.dailyLimit,
        )
    }

    private fun longestConsecutiveDays(dates: List<LocalDate>): Int {
        if (dates.isEmpty()) return 0

        var longest = 1
        var current = 1
        dates.zipWithNext().forEach { (previous, next) ->
            if (previous.plusDays(1) == next) {
                current += 1
                longest = maxOf(longest, current)
            } else {
                current = 1
            }
        }
        return longest
    }

    private data class RoomMissionProgress(
        val memoCount: Int,
        val isTodayDiaryComplete: Boolean,
        val weeklyDiaryCount: Int,
        val weeklyStreak: Int,
    )
}

object MissionEventPrefs {
    private const val PREF_NAME = "mission_event_prefs"
    private const val KEY_FRIEND_DIARY_VIEW = "friend_diary_view"
    private const val KEY_AD_VIEW_COUNT = "ad_view_count"
    private const val KEY_PROCESSED_AD_REWARDS = "processed_ad_rewards"
    private const val KEY_WEEKLY_SUMMARY_CREATED = "weekly_summary_created"
    private const val KEY_WEEKLY_SUMMARY_VIEWED = "weekly_summary_viewed"
    private const val KEY_WEEKLY_SUMMARY_REWARDED = "weekly_summary_rewarded"

    fun markFriendDiaryViewed(context: Context) {
        saveBoolean(context, KEY_FRIEND_DIARY_VIEW, koreaToday().toString())
    }

    fun markWeeklySummaryCreated(context: Context, summaryId: String) {
        addToSet(context, KEY_WEEKLY_SUMMARY_CREATED, summaryId)
    }

    fun markWeeklySummaryViewed(context: Context, summaryId: String) {
        if (summaryId in readSet(context, KEY_WEEKLY_SUMMARY_CREATED)) {
            addToSet(context, KEY_WEEKLY_SUMMARY_VIEWED, summaryId)
        }
    }

    fun hasFriendDiaryView(context: Context, date: LocalDate): Boolean =
        readBoolean(context, KEY_FRIEND_DIARY_VIEW, date.toString())

    fun getAdViewCount(context: Context, date: LocalDate): Int =
        context.getSharedPreferences(PREF_NAME, Context.MODE_PRIVATE)
            .getInt(scopedKey("${KEY_AD_VIEW_COUNT}_${date}"), 0)

    fun incrementAdViewCount(context: Context, date: LocalDate): Int {
        val nextCount = getAdViewCount(context, date) + 1
        context.getSharedPreferences(PREF_NAME, Context.MODE_PRIVATE)
            .edit()
            .putInt(scopedKey("${KEY_AD_VIEW_COUNT}_${date}"), nextCount)
            .apply()
        return nextCount
    }

    fun isAdRewardProcessed(context: Context, rewardId: String): Boolean =
        rewardId in readSet(context, KEY_PROCESSED_AD_REWARDS)

    fun markAdRewardProcessed(context: Context, rewardId: String) {
        addToSet(context, KEY_PROCESSED_AD_REWARDS, rewardId)
    }

    fun getWeeklySummaryRewardState(context: Context): WeeklySummaryRewardState {
        val created = readSet(context, KEY_WEEKLY_SUMMARY_CREATED)
        val viewed = readSet(context, KEY_WEEKLY_SUMMARY_VIEWED)
        val rewarded = readSet(context, KEY_WEEKLY_SUMMARY_REWARDED)
        val ready = created.intersect(viewed).minus(rewarded).sorted().firstOrNull()
        val latest = created.maxOrNull()

        return WeeklySummaryRewardState(
            readySummaryId = ready,
            latestSummaryRewarded = latest != null && latest in rewarded,
        )
    }

    fun markWeeklySummaryRewarded(context: Context, summaryId: String) {
        addToSet(context, KEY_WEEKLY_SUMMARY_REWARDED, summaryId)
    }

    private fun saveBoolean(context: Context, event: String, period: String) {
        context.getSharedPreferences(PREF_NAME, Context.MODE_PRIVATE)
            .edit()
            .putBoolean(scopedKey("${event}_$period"), true)
            .apply()
    }

    private fun readBoolean(context: Context, event: String, period: String): Boolean =
        context.getSharedPreferences(PREF_NAME, Context.MODE_PRIVATE)
            .getBoolean(scopedKey("${event}_$period"), false)

    private fun addToSet(context: Context, key: String, value: String) {
        val values = readSet(context, key).toMutableSet().apply { add(value) }
        context.getSharedPreferences(PREF_NAME, Context.MODE_PRIVATE)
            .edit()
            .putStringSet(scopedKey(key), values)
            .apply()
    }

    private fun readSet(context: Context, key: String): Set<String> =
        context.getSharedPreferences(PREF_NAME, Context.MODE_PRIVATE)
            .getStringSet(scopedKey(key), emptySet())
            ?.toSet()
            .orEmpty()

    private fun scopedKey(key: String): String = "user_${SessionManager.getUserId()}_$key"

    data class WeeklySummaryRewardState(
        val readySummaryId: String?,
        val latestSummaryRewarded: Boolean,
    )
}

private object MissionStatePrefs {
    private const val PREF_NAME = "mission_claim_prefs"

    fun isClaimed(
        context: Context,
        definition: MissionDefinition,
        today: LocalDate,
        weekStart: LocalDate,
    ): Boolean = context.getSharedPreferences(PREF_NAME, Context.MODE_PRIVATE)
        .getBoolean(scopedClaimKey(definition, today, weekStart), false)

    fun markClaimed(
        context: Context,
        definition: MissionDefinition,
        today: LocalDate,
        weekStart: LocalDate,
    ) {
        context.getSharedPreferences(PREF_NAME, Context.MODE_PRIVATE)
            .edit()
            .putBoolean(scopedClaimKey(definition, today, weekStart), true)
            .apply()
    }

    private fun scopedClaimKey(
        definition: MissionDefinition,
        today: LocalDate,
        weekStart: LocalDate,
    ): String = "user_${SessionManager.getUserId()}_${claimKey(definition, today, weekStart)}"

    private fun claimKey(
        definition: MissionDefinition,
        today: LocalDate,
        weekStart: LocalDate,
    ): String {
        val period = when (definition.category) {
            MissionCategory.DAILY -> today.toString()
            MissionCategory.WEEKLY, MissionCategory.REWARD -> weekStart.toString()
        }
        return "claimed_${definition.code}_$period"
    }
}

private fun koreaToday(): LocalDate = LocalDate.now(ZoneId.of("Asia/Seoul"))


========== FILE: C:\Users\SAMSUNG\mingu_github\sumdays\Sumdays\app\src\main\java\com\example\sumdays\mission\NetworkMissionRepository.kt ==========
package com.example.sumdays.mission

import android.content.Context
import androidx.work.BackoffPolicy
import androidx.work.Constraints
import androidx.work.ExistingWorkPolicy
import androidx.work.NetworkType
import androidx.work.OneTimeWorkRequestBuilder
import androidx.work.WorkManager
import androidx.work.workDataOf
import com.example.sumdays.auth.SessionManager
import com.example.sumdays.data.sync.BackupSyncRunner
import com.example.sumdays.data.sync.InitialSyncWorker
import com.example.sumdays.network.ApiClient
import com.example.sumdays.shop.PointPrefs
import java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit

class NetworkMissionRepository(context: Context) : MissionRepository {
    private val appContext = context.applicationContext
    private var cachedAdState = AdRewardState(
        watchedCount = 0,
        dailyLimit = MissionConfig.AD_DAILY_LIMIT,
        rewardPoints = MissionConfig.AD_REWARD,
    )

    override suspend fun getMissions(): List<Mission> {
        val session = requireSession()
        prepareServerState(session)
        val response = ApiClient.api.getMissionDashboard(session.authorization)
        if (!response.isSuccessful) {
            throw IllegalStateException("미션 조회 실패: ${response.code()}")
        }
        val dashboard = response.body()?.data
            ?: throw IllegalStateException("미션 응답 데이터가 없습니다.")

        ensureCurrent(session)
        PointPrefs.savePoint(appContext, session.userId, dashboard.pointBalance)
        cachedAdState = dashboard.adReward.toDomain()
        return dashboard.missions.map { it.toDomain() }
    }

    override suspend fun claimReward(missionCode: String): Int? {
        val session = requireSession()
        prepareServerState(session)
        val response = ApiClient.api.claimMissionReward(session.authorization, missionCode)
        if (!response.isSuccessful) {
            throw IllegalStateException("미션 보상 지급 실패: ${response.code()}")
        }
        val reward = response.body()?.data ?: return null
        ensureCurrent(session)
        PointPrefs.savePoint(appContext, session.userId, reward.pointBalance)
        return reward.rewardPoints
    }

    override fun getAdRewardState(): AdRewardState = cachedAdState

    override suspend fun claimAdReward(rewardId: String): AdRewardResult {
        val session = requireSession()
        initializeWallet(session)
        val response = ApiClient.api.claimAdReward(
            session.authorization,
            AdRewardClaimRequest(rewardId),
        )
        ensureCurrent(session)
        if (response.code() == 409) return AdRewardResult.DailyLimitReached
        if (!response.isSuccessful) {
            throw IllegalStateException("광고 보상 지급 실패: ${response.code()}")
        }
        val reward = response.body()?.data
            ?: throw IllegalStateException("광고 보상 응답 데이터가 없습니다.")
        PointPrefs.savePoint(appContext, session.userId, reward.pointBalance)
        cachedAdState = AdRewardState(
            watchedCount = reward.watchedCount ?: cachedAdState.watchedCount,
            dailyLimit = reward.dailyLimit ?: cachedAdState.dailyLimit,
            rewardPoints = reward.rewardPoints,
        )
        return AdRewardResult.Granted(
            rewardPoints = reward.rewardPoints,
            watchedCount = cachedAdState.watchedCount,
            dailyLimit = cachedAdState.dailyLimit,
        )
    }

    private fun MissionResponse.toDomain(): Mission {
        val missionCategory = runCatching { MissionCategory.valueOf(category) }
            .getOrDefault(MissionCategory.DAILY)
        val missionStatus = runCatching { MissionStatus.valueOf(status) }
            .getOrDefault(MissionStatus.IN_PROGRESS)
        return Mission(
            definition = MissionDefinition(
                code = code,
                category = missionCategory,
                title = title,
                description = description,
                targetCount = targetCount,
                rewardPoints = rewardPoints,
                displayOrder = displayOrder,
            ),
            currentCount = currentCount,
            status = missionStatus,
        )
    }

    private fun AdRewardResponse.toDomain() = AdRewardState(
        watchedCount = watchedCount,
        dailyLimit = dailyLimit,
        rewardPoints = rewardPoints,
    )

    private suspend fun prepareServerState(session: AuthSession) {
        initializeWallet(session)
        val result = BackupSyncRunner.syncNow(appContext)
        if (!result.success) {
            throw IllegalStateException(result.message ?: "최신 데이터를 동기화하지 못했습니다.")
        }
        ensureCurrent(session)
    }

    private suspend fun initializeWallet(session: AuthSession) {
        val response = ApiClient.api.initializePointWallet(
            session.authorization,
            LegacyPointBalanceRequest(
                PointPrefs.getLegacyPointForMigration(appContext, session.userId)
            ),
        )
        if (!response.isSuccessful) {
            throw IllegalStateException("포인트 지갑 준비 실패: ${response.code()}")
        }
        val balance = response.body()?.data?.pointBalance
            ?: throw IllegalStateException("포인트 지갑 응답 데이터가 없습니다.")
        ensureCurrent(session)
        PointPrefs.savePoint(appContext, session.userId, balance)
        PointPrefs.markLegacyPointHandled(appContext, session.userId)
    }

    private fun requireSession(): AuthSession {
        val userId = SessionManager.getUserId()
        val token = SessionManager.getToken()
        if (userId < 0 || token == null) {
            throw IllegalStateException("로그인이 필요합니다.")
        }
        return AuthSession(userId, "Bearer $token")
    }

    private fun ensureCurrent(session: AuthSession) {
        if (SessionManager.getUserId() != session.userId) {
            throw IllegalStateException("요청 처리 중 로그인 계정이 변경되었습니다.")
        }
    }

    private data class AuthSession(
        val userId: Int,
        val authorization: String,
    )
}

object MissionRepositoryFactory {
    fun create(context: Context): MissionRepository =
        if (MissionConfig.USE_SERVER_MISSIONS) {
            NetworkMissionRepository(context)
        } else {
            LocalMissionRepository(context)
        }
}

object MissionEventReporter {
    fun recordFriendDiaryView(context: Context, friendId: Int, diaryDate: String) {
        MissionEventPrefs.markFriendDiaryViewed(context)
        if (MissionConfig.USE_SERVER_MISSIONS) {
            enqueue(
                context = context,
                eventType = MissionEventWorker.EVENT_FRIEND_DIARY,
                eventKey = diaryDate,
                friendId = friendId,
                uniqueKey = "friend:$friendId:$diaryDate",
            )
        }
    }

    fun recordWeeklySummaryView(context: Context, summaryStartDate: String) {
        MissionEventPrefs.markWeeklySummaryViewed(context, summaryStartDate)
        if (MissionConfig.USE_SERVER_MISSIONS) {
            enqueue(
                context = context,
                eventType = MissionEventWorker.EVENT_WEEKLY_SUMMARY,
                eventKey = summaryStartDate,
                uniqueKey = "summary:$summaryStartDate",
            )
        }
    }

    private fun enqueue(
        context: Context,
        eventType: String,
        eventKey: String,
        uniqueKey: String,
        friendId: Int = -1,
    ) {
        val userId = SessionManager.getUserId()
        if (userId < 0) return

        val request = OneTimeWorkRequestBuilder<MissionEventWorker>()
            .setInputData(
                workDataOf(
                    MissionEventWorker.KEY_EVENT_TYPE to eventType,
                    MissionEventWorker.KEY_EVENT_KEY to eventKey,
                    MissionEventWorker.KEY_FRIEND_ID to friendId,
                    MissionEventWorker.KEY_EXPECTED_USER_ID to userId,
                )
            )
            .setConstraints(
                Constraints.Builder()
                    .setRequiredNetworkType(NetworkType.CONNECTED)
                    .build()
            )
            .setBackoffCriteria(BackoffPolicy.EXPONENTIAL, 10, TimeUnit.SECONDS)
            .addTag(InitialSyncWorker.accountWorkTag(userId))
            .build()

        WorkManager.getInstance(context.applicationContext).enqueueUniqueWork(
            "mission_event:$userId:$uniqueKey",
            ExistingWorkPolicy.KEEP,
            request,
        )
    }
}
